# Person given names and common surnames (NFC uppercase).
# Whole-lemma match only — never substring (BANANA must not match ANNA).
# Hangman length 3–10; Latin letters used in EN/DE/ES/PT packs.
#
# Sources (CC0):
# - Wikidata given-name items (P31 ∈ Q202444, Q12308941, Q11879590, Q3409032)
#   and family-name items (P31 = Q101352) with labels in en/de/es/pt.
#   Family names capped by sitelinks (>= 8) and LIMIT 4000 per language.
#   https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/Wikidata:Licensing
# - US SSA national baby names (names with ≥5 births/year), catalogued CC0 1.0
#   on data.gov. SSA names.zip 403s from some networks; this file was seeded
#   from the same public national records (1880–present).
#   https://catalog.data.gov/dataset/baby-names-from-social-security-card-applications-national-data
# - Pack residuals still reported with a person-name gloss (diminutives / fragments).
#
# RYAN and JOEY live here (not in the NSFW denylist).
